# Every distance-3 factory class with at most 48 injections

**4600 distinct `(n, k, S_k gate)` classes**, one verified witness circuit each: the 393 rows of `../../n40/catalog/classification_upto_n40.json` (n <= 40, copied verbatim) followed by the 4207 rows of `classification_n41_48.json` (best-a3 witnesses).

At n = 47, 48 the parents of the unique affine-rank-6 weight-48 word (check rank 6, and its rank-7 lift at n = 48) are not classified; every other check rank is.  See `CLASSIFICATION_N41_48.md` for the sector-by-sector statement.
